plan-logistics.md §3 (draft)
EasyNo duty on industrial products, including all apparel, footwear and bags, since 2024-01-01Import VAT waived when the VAT amount is at most CHF 5 (goods up to about CHF 62)8.1% VAT, on the price plus transportMail-order rule: above the threshold we register and charge Swiss VAT on all sales to CHSmall consignments into CH reach CHF 100,000 a yearNone for online sales
